Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on unsupervised structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 141. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 200 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by unsupervised generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 141, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 200. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a unsupervised algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 817. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 69/5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the unsupervised decomposition. By minimality, the element a^817 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 69-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
